# Skills · J · part 11

830 entries, jev-growth to jho-render. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **jev-growth**: Growth recipes built on Jev (TypeSafe AI's cheap decision model) for SEO and B2B agencies - automated SEO internal linking (one call per page, reader-click question, gap rule, anchors chosen from on-page text, near-duplicate detection), finding prospects who are ready to buy in public posts (buying, pain and vendor questions), checking every outreach message for fit and reply before it is sent, and Clay-style ICP lead lists that judge companies before paying to enrich them (Treg pattern). - **jev-guardrail**: Screen text or agent tool calls with a battery of local System One Noul hazards plus a Score, with policy in code — and the Claude Code PreToolUse guard (hooks/pretooluse-guard) that puts deterministic hard rules first and lets Jev only raise friction. Use for injection/secret/destructive-action screening or when the user runs /jev-guardrail. - **jev-guardrail"**: Uses the Jev System One model to score the risk of a shell command or file edit before it runs, returning a calibrated probability plus a three-way gate (allow, confirm, block). Invoke when a command is plausibly destructive (recursive delete, truncate, dd, mass rewrite, unreviewed pipe to shell) and the approval gate alone cannot tell how risky it is. Never blocks on its own — the calling agent applies the gate in code. ([EmanuProds/dotfiles](https://github.com/EmanuProds/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/skills/jev-guardrail) · 2 copies)

- **jev-honest**: Honest Jev — the claims-vs-evidence layer. Before saying done, fixed, works, verified, tested, safe or "should be fine", classify your own claims with jev_classify into verified / hedged / unverifiable / misrepresented and only send what you can show. Levels off / soft / standard / strict, switched with "/honest-jev soft|standard|strict|off" (also "honest-jev strict"); the active level is injected every session by the plugin hooks and shown as a statusline badge. Also runs an on-demand audit of a draft reply or recent claims ("be honest with me", "audit your last answer"). ([terenceooi99/token-saver-rtk-ide](https://github.com/terenceooi99/token-saver-rtk-ide/tree/HEAD/skills/jevgraph-query) · ★1)
- **jevgraph-query**: Inspect, query, or outline evidence-backed relations from a generated JevGraph knowledge graph or Cypher export. Saves 85-95% of prompt tokens vs reading raw 50-100 page document specs into LLM context. Activate when the user types /jevgraph-query, /jevgraph-inspect, "query graph", "inspect architecture graph", or asks to verify entity relations and page evidence. ([baysao/dot.agents](https://github.com/baysao/dot.agents/tree/HEAD/skills/jevgraph-query))
- **jevgrep**: Use for questions about how, why, or where behavior works in a repository, including questions that name a function or setting. Start behavioral discovery with jg before broad text searches or git history. When delegating repository discovery, instruct the subagent to start with jg. Jevgrep returns relevant files and source excerpts. For exact symbol definitions, string matches, or filenames, use grep or file search instead. ([dzhng/jevgrep](https://github.com/dzhng/jevgrep/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/jevgrep) · ★3.3k · 1.6k installs · 4 copies)
- **jevgrep**: Try one narrowly scoped Jevgrep (jg) search for unfamiliar code, with a 30-second limit, then use ordinary code search. ([RooCodeInc/Roomote](https://github.com/RooCodeInc/Roomote/tree/HEAD/packages/cloud-agents/src/server/workflows/skills/standard/jevgrep) · ★346)
- **jevgrep**: Operate Jevgrep (`jg`) as optional remote-agent discovery over explicitly scoped skills, wiki pages, or graph source documents. Use only when the user explicitly requests Jevgrep and separately approves transmitting source content and queries to the configured provider with potential cost. Preserve native catalog, exact/LSP/zvec retrieval and Graphify query/path/explain as defaults; Jevgrep does not query or rebuild durable graphs. ([akillness/jeo-skills](https://github.com/akillness/jeo-skills/tree/HEAD/.agent-skills/jevgrep) · ★60)
- **jevgrep**: Use Jevgrep (`jg` CLI) - `bun install --global @dzhng/jevgrep@latest` - to find relevant files and source excerpts from a natural-language repository question. ([loclv/agent-md](https://github.com/loclv/agent-md/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/jevgrep) · ★5)
- **jevgrep**: Use for questions about how, why or where behavior works in a codebase, when you do not yet know which files matter. Calls the code_find tool, which returns relevant files and verbatim excerpts. For a known symbol, string or filename use grep or read_file instead. ([Moissanite-Labs/exo](https://github.com/Moissanite-Labs/exo/tree/HEAD/skills/jevgrep) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **jevgrep**: リポジトリの中で、ある振る舞いがどこで・どう・なぜ動くのかを調べるときに使う。関数名や設定名が分かっている質問でも、振る舞いを探すなら広い文字列検索やgit履歴より先に使う。調査をサブエージェントに任せるときも、これを最初に使うよう指示に書く。決まった名前の定義・文字列・ファイル名を探すときは使わず、grepやファイル検索を使う ([masseater/cc-jev-teacher](https://github.com/masseater/cc-jev-teacher/tree/HEAD/plugins/cc-jev-pruner/skills/jevgrep) · ★1)
- **jevgrep**: Use jevgrep (jg) to locate where a behavior lives in unfamiliar code from a natural-language question. Use rg for exact names. ([AO-HyS/development-system](https://github.com/AO-HyS/development-system/tree/HEAD/artifacts/1.38.0/skills/external/jevgrep) · 2 copies)
- **jevgrep**: Use for how, why or where questions about a repository, including ones that name a function or setting. Start behavioral discovery with jg before broad text searches; tell delegated subagents to do the same. For exact symbols, strings or filenames use grep. ([DerekHertz/DimSumDev](https://github.com/DerekHertz/DimSumDev/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/jevgrep))
- **jevguard-rules**: Use when the user wants to create, add, edit, remove, weaken, or repair a JevGuard policy rule in `.jev/rules.md`, or asks what JevGuard checks. Do not use for `.jev/config.yaml`, credentials, or running a review. ([pablozr/JevGuard](https://github.com/pablozr/JevGuard/tree/HEAD/packages/plugin/skills/jevguard-rules) · ★1)
- **jeview**: jeview（ファイルごとに問題がありそうかを Jev に聞くコードレビュー CLI）を実行し、結果の JSON から先に読むべきファイルを選んで、コードを読んで確かめてから報告する。リポジトリ全体のレビュー、commit 前の変更の確認、PR の差分の確認を頼まれたとき、または jeview の結果（result.json）を読むときに使う。 ([YuSa0-6/jeviews](https://github.com/YuSa0-6/jeviews/tree/HEAD/skills/jeview) · ★2)
- **jevify**: Use Jev as System One to save the user reasoning-model tokens, time, and cost on bulk reading or repeated semantic decisions. Trigger when sifting large documents or tool outputs, selecting evidence or actions from many candidates, or checking many items against criteria, even if the user never mentions Jev. Also use for "Jevify this", Jev question design, and product work where frequent, inexpensive judgments could improve the experience. ([altryne/jevify](https://github.com/altryne/jevify/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★49 · 3 copies)
- **jevify**: Run /jevify to audit an app's runtime AI calls (repository or Lovable project) and flag structured decisions that fit JEV; the audit never edits source. - **jevify**: Audit and selectively convert existing AI workflows or agents to TypeSafe Jev, with user-approved scope and measured fallback validation. - **jevis**: Change what Jevis does for your coding agents. Add, edit, or turn off a tip, a lesson, the Stop check, or the design rules the reviewer uses, and prove the change works. - **jevis-improve**: Review what Jevis has been doing from its own records and the user's agent transcripts, then propose changes as a diff for the user to review. - **jevitate-ci-check**: Set up and read jevitate's CI regression gate. `jevitate check --suite &lt;file&gt;` runs promoted Journeys, invariants, goals, missions and verify-fix re-checks within a budget, writes JUnit + SARIF + JSON, and its exit code decides pass/fail. Also covers `--baseline`/`--changed-routes`, `jevitate report` (deduped defect list), `jevitate diff`, and `baseline tag`. Use when adding jevitate to CI, reading a failed check, or comparing runs. ([matt-cochran/jevitate](https://github.com/matt-cochran/jevitate/tree/HEAD/packages/skills/skills/jevitate-ci-check) · ★1)
- **jevitate-demo**: Make narrated product demos with jevitate. Covers one aspect from a one-line request (`jevitate demo "&lt;aspect&gt;" --env`, then the human's `demo approve`), an existing Journey replayed as a WebM video with .vtt subtitles and/or a Markdown guide with screenshots (`jevitate journey demo`), step intent drafts (`journey annotate`), and named environments (`--env`, .jevitate/environments.json). Use for a demo, walkthrough, onboarding video or how-to guide. Not for testing. ([matt-cochran/jevitate](https://github.com/matt-cochran/jevitate/tree/HEAD/packages/skills/skills/jevitate-demo) · ★1)
- **jevitate-explore**: Run a bounded jevitate exploration of an authorized web app. Strategies: goal (reach an end state, checked by `--success`), find-out (answer a question), coverage/exploratory (state coverage), adversarial (try to break it), feature (model-free), and usability. Runs via `jevitate explore` or MCP `run_exploration`/`queue_exploration`; saves a verified path as a Journey (`explore-author-journey`). Use when there's no saved Journey for what needs testing, or the user wants bugs found. ([matt-cochran/jevitate](https://github.com/matt-cochran/jevitate/tree/HEAD/packages/skills/skills/jevitate-explore) · ★1)
- **jevitate-getting-started**: Start here for any jevitate task, or when unsure which jevitate skill applies — checks setup (keys, .jevitate/, MCP), gets a first useful result against the user's app URL, routes to the right skill (explore, run-journey, record, demo, verify-fix, ci-check, ux-review, load-test, sources, mission-scope, test-campaign), and says when to use the jevitate MCP tools vs the CLI, what the exit codes mean, and which approvals only a human may give. ([matt-cochran/jevitate](https://github.com/matt-cochran/jevitate/tree/HEAD/packages/skills/skills/jevitate-getting-started) · ★1)

([matt-cochran/jevitate](https://github.com/matt-cochran/jevitate/tree/HEAD/packages/skills/skills/jevitate-load-test) · ★1)
- **jevitate-mission-scope**: Turn a diff, pull request, changelog or user story into targeted jevitate testing. - **jevitate-record**: Record a flow the human clicks through (`jevitate record --url`, CLI only, headed), then post-process the takes: propose an unpromoted draft Journey from 2+ takes, diff them, classify values (postdoc), fit a timing policy, promote a value to a variable (`jevitate recording ...`, MCP `recordings`). Covers add/list/pull/update/remove (`jevitate source ...`, MCP `sources`), human-only trust of a single Journey (content-hash-bound), running a trusted remote Journey through the run-gate (`source run`), and publishing a promoted local Journey (`journey publish`, MCP `publish_journey`). Use for third-party or shared Journeys. For a Journey already in this repo, use jevitate-run-journey. - **jevitate-test-campaign**: Plan and run an end-to-end release test campaign of a whole web app with jevitate: preflight, a jobs-to-be-done catalog, discovery runs → draft Journeys, journey-anchored adversarial/exploratory missions, full-stack evidence, triage by exit code, verify-fix and regressions, budgets, CI suites and the release gate. Use to test a release or "the whole app" across many user jobs. For one run, use that tool's skill. - **jevitate-ux-review**: Produce ranked, cited, advisory usability findings (preview) for a flow — offline over a saved Recording (`jevitate ux &lt;recording&gt; --app-class`, MCP `ux_review`) or live on an authorized target (`jevitate explore --strategy usability --goal --app-class`, MCP `run_exploration`). - **jevitate-verify-fix**: Reproduce a jevitate finding by its fingerprint and prove a fix (`jevitate verify-fix`, MCP `verify_fix`); attach evidence (`--evidence-video` clips, `--record-video` before/after, `--screenshots`); keep findings re-checkable (`jevitate ledger`); lock a bug in as a committed regression (`jevitate regression capture|run`). - **jevlogs**: Use Jev (TypeSafe's structured evaluation model, via the Vercel AI SDK) to score, prioritize, and selectively route application logs with the open-source jevlogs npm package.
